- **task-update-open-loops**: Writes calendar flags (upcoming deadline clusters, focus time deficits, unscheduled high-priority items) to vault/calendar/open-loops.md and resolves completed items. ([fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life](https://github.com/fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life/tree/HEAD/calendar/skills/task-update-open-loops) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **task-update-open-loops**: The single write point for vault/health/open-loops.md. Called at the end of every health op and flow. Appends new flags (lab anomalies, wearable deviations, refill reminders, preventive care gaps) with timestamp, source op, and severity. Scans existing entries for items that can be auto-resolved — expired refill reminders, lab flags where the biomarker returned to normal, care gaps that have been completed. Preserves resolved items with resolution date for audit trail. ([fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life](https://github.com/fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life/tree/HEAD/health/skills/task-update-open-loops) · ★17 · 2 copies)
